PCW — Pay-per-view

Red October 2002, 6 October 2002

6 October 2002
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
Red October 2002 6 Oct 2002 The Tokyo Dome Red October opening montage As the PCW Red October graphic fades out, the soaring, string-laden opening of David Arnold’s composition “Play Dead” blares out. A THIRD DAWNING GAVE BIRTH TO THE THIRD AGE.... A bloodied Xavier Kannon is then shown holding the PCW World Title aloft over the fallen body of Tyler Nelson. BUT FAR TO THE EAST.... The giant Akiyama is shown removing his cloak. IN THE LAND OF THE RISING SUN..... We see Miyagawa leap up high then down onto Duke Williams with the Five Elements Frog Splash. A NEW DAWN MAY BE UPON US.... The stern face of Egypt’s King, the Pharaoh is shown face to face with the Neo Void God. EIGHT MEN..... The fierce yet conceited grin of the Villain’ Villain, Snow looks right at the Mexican Flag adorned eyes of El Diablo. FIVE MATCHES..... The goggled eyes of Miyagawa lock onto the smug face of PCW’s President, Duke Williams. FIFTY FIVE THOUSAND FANS..... The snarling face of Clyde burns holes on the smirking face of PCW Champion Xavier Kannon. ONE CHAMPION. As the shot of Kannon focuses on the World Title belt held over his shoulder the strings give way to the raw, over driven guitars of “Rocket Science” by the Pimps. On the shiny plate of the belt, images of the 8 men in battle are shown, each on transparent, mapped over the words.... PCW WORLD HEAVYWEIGHT CHAMPION. The images of the 8 give way as the belt counts back images of past PCW Champions.... From Frisco then Big Alan Steele, to Meanstreak then Kremmen before the reflections are lost and the shot is drenched with intense White light and the deafening sounds of explosions. Commentary team introduction “HELLO AND WELCOME TO PCW’S RED OCTOBER!!!!” The bright glare dissipates to show a high view from way up in the Tokyo Dome where all around the giant arena White and Red pyro is going off. In the ring, along the aisle, on the stage, from the JumboTron, from the rafters and lighting rig... All accompanied by an enormous pop from the Japanese crowd as “Rocket Science” continues to blare out. “AND WHAT AN EVENT WE HAVE FOR YOU TONIGHT! WE HAVE SIXTEEN MATCHES... EVERY PCW TITLE BELT IS ON THE LINE TONIGHT AND THE VERY HONOUR OF THE COMPANY ITSELF AS PRIMETIME CHAMPIONSHIP WRESTLING SETS FOOT ON THE TURF OF PURE!!!” After the camera completes it’s final lap of the crowd, the camera comes to the special Pay Per View announce team of Dave Kern, Jeff Marx, and in Duke Williams’ seat none other than the Pimply Faced Intern Kid. Dave: From me, Dave Kern. Jeff Marx, and our special guest the Pimply Faced Intern Kid allow me to welcome you too the Tokyo Dome where tonight we see what is shaping up to be the biggest PCW show of not just the Third Age... But of the company’s very history! Jeff: That’s right, Kern. And you know who we have to thank for it? THE FRONT OFFICE! PFIK: The- Jeff: SHUT UP! Dave: Tonight we see 8 men who could theoretically walk out with the PCW Title around their waist as for the first time ever.... PCW OPENS a PPV with a World Title contest, and an INFERNO match to boot! Jeff: BAH~! TWO men, Kern... TWO MEN! DA BOSS~! or DA PREZ! Dave: Well, with Akiyama and Miy- Jeff: I SAID TWO MEN!!! Dave is silent.... Jeff: Dave? Dave shakes... Jeff: Oh Hubbard, he’s Kerning Up!!! Dave: Fucking shit, we are ready to start with the WORLD TITLE MATCH!!! Jeff: *Sigh* Here we go.... Which also happens to be an INFERNO MATCH!!! PFIK: Uh, I can’t stand high temperatures! I get all sweaty and my skin starts to break out, especially on my forehead… Jeff: I wouldn’t worry; I don’t think there is a clear patch of skin on you body… PFIK: I…Uh…that’s not nice! Jeff: You want to make something of it, kiddo!?!? PFIK: (Quivering in fear) Uh…I…I’ll stand over here on my side, and you promise not to hurt me… Dave: Come on kid, stand up for yourself! Marx is a fucking pansy! PFIK lowers his head, trying not to make eye contact with Jeff. Dave sighs. Dave: This match has been in the making ever since Kannon fired DarkStar... Not the best thing to do when Clyde already bagged driving the Goth from PCW! Jeff: BAH~! Clyde can join his other DarkAge morons Ty*Beep*son, DarkStar and Stryfe in the bush leagues... Or street corners! Dave: We’ll see... This match is highly stacked in Clyde’s favour... Jeff: Yeah, so he’ll look even more retarded when he blows it. Dave: Well let’s hand this one over to Clay before the Intern Kid replaces Marx’s blowup doll… Jeff: Hey, I may be a perverted sex-starved male, but I’m not gay…anymore! Dave sighs again. Clyde vs Xavier Kannon (c) Clay: The following contest is AN INFERNO MATCH AND IS FOR THE PCW WORLD HEAVYWEIGHT TITLE!!! “Seasons in the Abyss” by Slayer plays over the loudspeakers as Clyde along with Bethany make their way to the ring. Clay: Introducing first, being accompanied by BETHANY…from Texas, weighing in at 270 pounds…THE SICKEST MAN IN THE PCW, hell… THE WORLD… CLLLLLLLLLLYYYYYYYDDDDDDEEEEE!!! Clyde steps into the ring and pulls out a lighter. He sticks the lighter near his arm and lights up, singeing all the hair on his arm! Clyde’s eyes begin to roll up as if he was in some orgasmic trance… Jeff: He is fucking sick! Look at that! Dave: At least he isn’t putting out the fire by bashing his arm with a live wolverine… Jeff rolls his eyes. “Rock is Dead” by Marilyn Manson plays over the PA system, as Xavier Kannon and Ellie walk down the aisle. Clay: And now…being accompanied by ELLIE~! From Hubbardville, Wisconsin, weighing in at 229 pounds…he is the PCW WORLD CHAMPION…XAVIIIEEEEERRRRRRR KAN-NOOOONNNN!!! Kannon struts into the ring and holds his title into the air. Crowd: BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO!!! Jeff: Peons! Ingrates! Morons! Show Kannon the respect he deserves!!! Dave: He doesn’t deserve any respect Marx; he’s weaselled his way through the ranks of the PCW, and finally will get some comeuppance! PFIK: I have to agree with Kern on this one… Jeff: (Interrupting) What did you fucking say pip-squeak!?!? PFIK: Um…nothing… Dave sighs. Jeff: That’s right, you said your mom was a whore! Didn’t you!?!? PFIK: … Dave: Stand up dammit! PFIK: I…I…I did… Dave sighs again. PCW ring crewmembers begin to douse the ropes and ring apron with gasoline. A ring crewmember lights a match and throws it at the ropes, but nothing happens. Another ring crewmember rubs two twigs together by the ropes but is unsuccessful at creating a fire. Jeff: Anyone got any flint!?!? Suddenly, to the amazement of the crowd, a young woman that looks incredibly like Drew Barrymore walks down the aisle towards the ring. HOLY SHIT! Jeff: (Tears coming from his eyes) Thank you Fire Starter!!! Dark Gimmick signals for the bell…DING! DING! DING! Dave: Let’s get this party started!!! Kannon immediately rushes at Clyde, who ducks a clothesline from Hubbard’s Main Man, and fires back with a HUGE RIGHT HAND, dropping Kannon back down to the canvas! Jeff: Ack!!! Right in his Hubbard-Given Nose!!! Crowd: OOOOOOOOOoooooo!!! Kannon quickly rolls back up to his feet, he points at Clyde and makes a throat-slashing gesture. Clyde looks at Kannon and snickers. Dave: You’re right kid, all Clyde has to do is catch on fire and his hopes of winning the World Title are gone! The crowd begins to clap and chant “Clyde!” in order to instil a rush of adrenaline into Da Man. Crowd: CLYDE! CLYDE! CLYDE! CLYDE! CLYDE! CLYDE! Bethany cheers her man on, as does Ellie. Jeff: Boy if I had those two little pieces of ass I’d… PFIK: Turn them down for one night with Harvey Fierstein??? Jeff: Yeah I’d…hey…WAIT A SECOND!!! Dave: Ha! Good one kid! The Crowd claps in unison, trying to bring Da Man back to his feet… Clyde slowly gets to his knees as Kannon continues to hold onto him. Jeff: That’s right Xavier old boy, don’t let go! But Kannon cannot contain the rush of evil energy inside Clyde. Da Man gets to his feet, and elbows Kannon in the gut…AGAIN!!! AGAIN!!! AGAIN!!! HE’S FINALLY FREE!!! Crowd: YAY!!! Clyde quickly runs to the ropes and ALMOST IMMEDIATELY PUTS THE BRAKES ON!!! He looks at the crowd and shakes his pointer finger.
